The arithmetic is shown; no calculator is needed.</p>	<span style='font-size:85%;'>6&minus;2 =</span> <strong>4</strong>	Incorrect	<span style='font-size:85%;'>6&minus;1 =</span> <strong>5</strong>	Correct	<span style='font-size:85%;'>6&minus;0 =</span> <strong>6</strong>	Incorrect	<span style='font-size:85%;'>114&minus;1 =</span> <strong>113</strong>	Incorrect

The arithmetic is shown; no calculator is needed.</p>	<span style='font-size:85%;'>6&minus;2 =</span> <strong>4</strong>	Incorrect	<span style='font-size:85%;'>6&minus;1 =</span> <strong>5</strong>	Correct	<span style='font-size:85%;'>6&minus;0 =</span> <strong>6</strong>	Incorrect	<span style='font-size:85%;'>30&minus;1 =</span> <strong>29</strong>	Incorrect
